- **lxd-docker-firewall-conflict**: Diagnose and fix the well-known Docker/LXD firewall conflict on a host running both. Docker sets the iptables FORWARD chain policy to DROP and accepts only its own bridges, so forwarded traffic from the LXD bridge (lxdbr0) is silently dropped and LXD containers/VMs get no outbound internet (the host itself is fine). Fix: accept the LXD bridge in the DOCKER-USER chain, then persist it with a systemd unit ordered after docker.service. Use when an LXD container has no internet or cannot reach archive.ubuntu.com, when "apt update"/"apt-get"/"curl" inside an LXD container times out or reports "Net… ([soulmachine/skills](https://github.com/soulmachine/skills/tree/HEAD/lxd-docker-firewall-conflict) · ★3)
